function startAuth(){FB().onAuthStateChanged(window.auth,user=>{handleAuthUser(user).catch(e=>{console.error('Auth observer:',e);showLogin('Não foi possível abrir a sessão. Tente novamente.')})})}

function refreshIdentityUI(){
  const chip=$('#idChip'),av=$('#idAv'),nm=$('#idName'),me=resolveCurrentMember();
  const displayName=me?.nome||authProfile?.nome||authUser?.email||'Entrar';
  nm.textContent=displayName;
  av.innerHTML=me?.foto?`<img src="${esc(me.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(displayName);
  av.style.background=me?(NIVEIS[me.nivel]?.color||'var(--ink-3)'):'var(--ink-3)';
  chip.classList.toggle('admin',isAdmin);
  if($('#whoName')) $('#whoName').textContent=displayName;
  if($('#whoDot')) $('#whoDot').style.background=me?(NIVEIS[me.nivel]?.color||'var(--ink-3)'):'var(--ink-3)';
  $('#adminBtn').style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  $('#adminBtn').title='Gerenciar acessos';
  $('#lockIcon').innerHTML=isAdmin?'<path d="M12 3 4 7v5c0 5 3.4 8.5 8 9 4.6-.5 8-4 8-9V7z"/><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"/>':'';
  updateAdminButtons();
}
function updateAdminButtons(){
  const add=$('#addBtn'); if(add){add.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none'; add.title=isAdmin?'Adicionar aluno à rede':'Somente coordenador';}
  const ev=$('#newEventBtn'); if(ev)ev.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  const ml=$('#manageLinhasBtn'); if(ml)ml.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  const rc=$('#recadoBtn'); if(rc)rc.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  const pb=$('#peopleAdminBlock'); if(pb)pb.style.display=isAdmin?'block':'none';
  const mp=$('#managePeopleBtn'); if(mp)mp.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
}
function refreshMemberDependentUI(){
  refreshIdentityUI(); const cn=$('#cNivel'),cur=cn.value; cn.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os níveis</option>'+Object.entries(NIVEIS).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}">${v.label}</option>`).join(''); cn.value=cur;
  if(isAdmin) populateAccessMemberSelects();
}
function openIdentity(){
  const me=resolveCurrentMember(), name=me?.nome||authProfile?.nome||authUser?.email||'';
  $('#accountName').textContent=name; $('#accountEmail').textContent=authUser?.email||''; $('#accountRole').textContent=isAdmin?'Coordenador':'Carbonauta';
  const link=$('#accountLink'); if(link)link.textContent=`UID: ${authUser?.uid||'—'} · Perfil: ${me?.id||myId||'não vinculado'}`;
  $('#accountRepairBtn').style.display=authUser?'inline-flex':'none';
  $('#accountAv').textContent=initials(name); $('#accountAv').style.background=me?(NIVEIS[me.nivel]?.color||'var(--petrol)'):'var(--petrol)'; openOverlay('idOverlay');
}
function populateAccessMemberSelects(){
  const linked=new Set((state.users||[]).map(u=>u.memberId)), opts=[...state.members].sort((a,b)=>a.nome.localeCompare(b.nome)).map(m=>`<option value="${m.id}" ${linked.has(m.id)?'disabled':''}>${esc(m.nome)}${linked.has(m.id)?' — já vinculado':''}</option>`).join('');
  ['accessMember','linkMember'].forEach(id=>{const el=$('#'+id);if(el)el.innerHTML=opts});
}
function openAccessManager(){if(!isAdmin){toast('Acesso restrito ao coordenador.');return}populateAccessMemberSelects();renderAccessList();openOverlay('pinOverlay')}

function openPersonalRepoSharing(){if(!myId)return;$('#personalRepoShareSearch').value='';renderPersonalRepoSharePeople();openOverlay('personalRepoShareOverlay')}
async function savePersonalRepoSharing(){await ensurePersonalRepositoryRoot();const btn=$('#personalRepoShareSaveBtn'),selected=[...document.querySelectorAll('#personalRepoShareList input:checked')].map(x=>x.value),old=new Set(repoRootCollaborators()),newly=selected.filter(id=>!old.has(id)),f=FB(),uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid,m=memberById(myId),rootRef=f.doc(window.db,'rede_personal_repositories',myId);try{btn.disabled=true;await f.setDoc(rootRef,{ownerUid:uid,ownerMemberId:myId,ownerName:m?.nome||myName||'',allowedMemberIds:[myId,...selected],collaboratorMemberIds:selected,updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});const mine=personalPackagesOwned(),batch=f.writeBatch(window.db);for(const p of mine){const extras=new Set(p.folderExtraMemberIds||((p.allowedMemberIds||[]).filter(id=>id!==myId&&!(p.rootSharedMemberIds||[]).includes(id))));let allowed=[];if(p.accessMode==='group')allowed=state.members.filter(x=>x.status!=='inativo').map(x=>x.id);else allowed=[...new Set([myId,...selected,...extras])];batch.update(f.doc(window.db,'rede_repository_packages',p.id),{allowedMemberIds:allowed,rootSharedMemberIds:selected,folderExtraMemberIds:[...extras],updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()})}if(mine.length)await batch.commit();await Promise.allSettled(newly.map(id=>createRepoNotification(id,{kind:'repositorio_compartilhado',sourceType:'repositorio_pessoal',sourceId:myId,ownerId:myId,title:'🔐 Repositório compartilhado com você',message:`${m?.nome||'Um Carbonauta'} deu acesso ao repositório pessoal.`})));closeOverlay('personalRepoShareOverlay');toast('Acessos do repositório atualizados.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'Publique as regras P22 no Firestore.':(e.message||'Não foi possível salvar os acessos.'))}finally{btn.disabled=false}}
function renderFolderAccessPeople(p){const q=($('#repoFolderAccessSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase(),selected=new Set(p?.folderExtraMemberIds||[]),rootIds=rootAllowedMemberIds(p?.ownerMemberId),wrap=$('#repoFolderAccessList');if(!wrap)return;wrap.innerHTML=state.members.filter(m=>m.status!=='inativo'&&m.id!==p.ownerMemberId&&(!q||m.nome.toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>a.nome.localeCompare(b.nome,'pt-BR')).map(m=>`<label class="repo-share-row"><input type="checkbox" value="${m.id}" ${selected.has(m.id)?'checked':''} ${rootIds.has(m.id)?'disabled':''}><span><b>${esc(m.nome)}</b><small>${rootIds.has(m.id)?'Já tem acesso ao repositório inteiro':'Acesso somente a esta pasta'}</small></span></label>`).join('')}

async function openNativePrivate(memberId,draft,name='Carbonauta',meta={}){
  if(busy)return;busy=true;
  try{
    const me=await currentProfile();if(!me?.memberId){toast('Seu acesso não está vinculado corretamente à Rede.');return}
    const dir=await ensureDirectory(memberId,name);if(!dir.ok){if(dir.reason==='no-account')toast(`${first(name)} ainda não tem conta vinculada. Use 🛡️ Gerenciar acessos.`);else if(dir.reason==='multiple')toast(`${first(name)} tem mais de uma conta vinculada. Corrija em 🛡️ Gerenciar acessos.`);else if(dir.reason==='permission')toast(`O vínculo privado de ${first(name)} está bloqueado pelas permissões. Revise em 🛡️ Gerenciar acessos.`);else toast(`Não consegui preparar a conversa de ${first(name)}.`);return}
    const tid=threadId(me.memberId,memberId);switchToPrivate();await sleep(160);
    let row=findThreadRow(tid);
    if(!row){
      const nb=document.getElementById('privateNewBtn');if(!nb){toast('A área de conversa privada ainda não carregou.');return}nb.click();
      let pb=null;for(let i=0;i<35;i++){pb=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-private-person]')].find(x=>x.dataset.privatePerson===memberId)||null;if(pb)break;await sleep(100)}
      if(!pb){document.getElementById('privateNewOverlay')?.classList.remove('open');toast(`${first(name)} tem conta, mas ainda não aparece em Nova conversa.`);return}
      if(!pb.disabled)pb.click();
      document.getElementById('privateNewOverlay')?.classList.remove('open');
      row=await waitThreadRow(tid,55);
      if(!row){toast(`A conversa com ${first(name)} não foi criada/recuperada. Revise o vínculo em Gerenciar acessos.`);return}
    }
    row.click();
    let input=null;for(let i=0;i<60;i++){input=document.getElementById('privateInput');if(input&&document.getElementById('privateShell')?.classList.contains('chat-open'))break;await sleep(100)}
    if(!input||!document.getElementById('privateShell')?.classList.contains('chat-open')){toast(`Não consegui abrir a conversa com ${first(name)}.`);return}
    closeActionLayers();switchToPrivate();await sleep(80);
    input.value=draft;input.focus();input.style.height='auto';input.style.height=Math.min(130,input.scrollHeight)+'px';input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));
    pending={...meta,memberId,personName:name,draft,openedAt:Date.now()};toast(`Conversa com ${first(name)} aberta. Revise e envie.`)
  }finally{busy=false}
}

async function loadData(){
  const f=F();if(!f||!window.db)return false;
  try{
    const [us,ms]=await Promise.all([f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_users')),f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'))]);
    usersCache=us.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),uid:d.id}));membersCache=ms.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));return true
  }catch(e){console.warn('P74 acesso administrativo',e);return false}
}
function memberName(id){return membersCache.find(m=>m.id===id)?.nome||usersCache.find(u=>u.memberId===id)?.nome||'Carbonauta'}

function fillUnlinked(){

}

async function refresh(force=false){
  const ov=document.getElementById('pinOverlay');if(!ov||(!force&&!ov.classList.contains('open'))||refreshing)return;refreshing=true;

}
function boot(){injectSection();const ov=document.getElementById('pinOverlay');if(ov)new MutationObserver(()=>refresh()).observe(ov,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class'],childList:true,subtree:true});document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('#adminBtn,#managePeopleBtn'))setTimeout(()=>refresh(true),180)},true);setInterval(()=>{if(document.getElementById('pinOverlay')?.classList.contains('open'))augmentResetButtons()},1200);console.info('Carbonautas',VERSION,BUILD,'senhas e acessos restaurados')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

function admin(){try{return !!isAdmin}catch(_e){return false}}

function ms(v){if(!v)return 0;try{if(typeof v.toMillis==='function')return v.toMillis();if(v.seconds)return v.seconds*1000;if(typeof v==='string'){if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v))return new Date(v+'T12:00:00').getTime();const t=Date.parse(v);return isNaN(t)?0:t}if(typeof v==='number')return v;if(v instanceof Date)return v.getTime()}catch(_e){}return 0}
function dayIso(d){const x=d||new Date();return x.getFullYear()+'-'+String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(x.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}
function fmtShort(t){if(!t)return'';return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})}
function ago(t){if(!t)return'nunca';const d=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/86400000);if(d<=0)return'hoje';if(d===1)return'ontem';if(d<30)return`${d} d`;const m=Math.floor(d/30);return m<12?`${m} m`:`${Math.floor(m/12)} a`}
function first(n){return String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/)[0]||'Carbonauta'}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 4) linha do tempo por pessoa ---------- */
const SHOW=12;
function timelineItems(m){
  const items=[];
  (m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.data);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:p.feito?'done':(t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`Prazo · ${p.titulo||''}`})});
  (st().activities||[]).filter(a=>a.ownerId===m.id).forEach(a=>{
    const t=ms(a.dueDate)||ms(a.updatedAt)||ms(a.createdAt);if(!t)return;
    const done=a.status==='concluido'||!!a.doneAt;
    items.push({t,cls:done?'done':(a.dueDate&&t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`${a.type==='produto'?'Produto':a.type==='orientacao'?'Orientação':'Tarefa'} · ${a.title||''}`})
  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Arquivo · ${p.titulo||p.fileName||''}${p.reviewFlow?' · v'+(p.reviewVersion||1):''}`})});
  (st().photos||[]).filter(p=>p.autorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.dataRealizacao)||ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Foto · ${p.legenda||p.projeto||''}`})});
  (st().feed||[]).filter(p=>p.authorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Destaque · ${p.title||p.text||''}`})});
  return items.sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
}

// P129 · bibliotecas pesadas so quando a tela precisa
const P129_LIBS={
  d3:{src:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/d3@7/dist/d3.min.js',ok:()=>!!window.d3},
  mammoth:{src:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/mammoth@1.10.0/mammoth.browser.min.js',ok:()=>!!window.mammoth},
  xlsx:{src:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js',ok:()=>!!window.XLSX},
  pdfjs:{src:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js',ok:()=>!!window.pdfjsLib}
};
const p129LibPromises={};
function ensureLib(name){
  const lib=P129_LIBS[name];if(!lib)return Promise.reject(new Error('lib desconhecida: '+name));
  if(lib.ok())return Promise.resolve();
  if(p129LibPromises[name])return p129LibPromises[name];
  p129LibPromises[name]=new Promise((res,rej)=>{const s=document.createElement('script');s.src=lib.src;s.async=true;s.onload=()=>lib.ok()?res():rej(new Error(name+' nao inicializou'));s.onerror=()=>{delete p129LibPromises[name];rej(new Error('Nao consegui baixar '+name+'. Verifique a conexao.'))};document.head.appendChild(s)});
  return p129LibPromises[name];
}
window.ensureLib=ensureLib;

const $$=s=>document.querySelectorAll(s);

function slugify(s){
  return 'seed-'+(s||'').toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-')
    .replace(/(^-|-$)/g,'');
}

function updateQuickBadge(){

}

  const filter=muralFilter||'all';

  if(filter==='recado') return item.__type==='recado';
  if(item.__type!=='post') return false;
  const k=item.kind||'atualizacao';
  if(filter==='ajuda') return k.startsWith('ajuda_');
  return k===filter;
}

  const unread=items.filter(c=>(c.ts?.toMillis?c.ts.toMillis():+new Date(c.ts||0))>lastSeenChat).length;
  const b=$('#muralBadge');
  if(currentView!=='mural' && unread){b.textContent=unread>9?'9+':unread;b.style.display='grid'} else b.style.display='none';
}

function muralMatches(item){
  const filter=muralFilter||'all';
  if(filter==='all') return true;
  if(filter==='recado') return item.__type==='recado';
  if(item.__type!=='post') return false;
  const k=item.kind||'atualizacao';
  if(filter==='ajuda') return k.startsWith('ajuda_');
  return k===filter;
}

async function renderMedals(){const mk=medalMonthKey();$('#medalMonth').textContent=medalMonthLabel();if(mk===monthKey()){medalLoadedKey=mk;medalLoadedPoints=state.points||[];renderMedalsFrom(medalLoadedPoints);return}if(medalLoadedKey===mk){renderMedalsFrom(medalLoadedPoints);return}$('#medalBoard').innerHTML='<div class="ag-empty">Carregando mês…</div>';try{const f=FB(),q=f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_points_events'),f.where('monthKey','==',mk)),snap=await f.getDocs(q);medalLoadedKey=mk;medalLoadedPoints=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderMedalsFrom(medalLoadedPoints)}catch(e){console.error(e);$('#medalBoard').innerHTML='<div class="ag-empty">Não foi possível carregar o ranking.</div>'}}
function shiftMedalMonth(n){medalCursor=new Date(medalCursor.getFullYear(),medalCursor.getMonth()+n,1);if(medalMonthKey()>monthKey())medalCursor=new Date(new Date().getFullYear(),new Date().getMonth(),1);medalLoadedKey='';renderMedals()}
function reportGameReward(score){const k=dayKey();awardPoints(myId,'game_complete',k,`Jogo completo · ${Math.round(Number(score)||0).toLocaleString('pt-BR')} pontos no jogo`).then(()=>{if(currentView==='medalhas')renderMedals()});toast('🏅 Ciclo completo: +10 pontos no ranking de hoje.')}

function muralTime(item){
  // Aceita Timestamp real do Firestore, objetos serializados e campos antigos.
  const candidates=[item?.ts,item?.createdAt,item?.updatedAt,item?.timestamp,item?.date];
  for(const t of candidates){
    if(!t) continue;
    if(typeof t?.toMillis==='function'){
      const v=t.toMillis(); if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0) return v;
    }
    const sec=t?.seconds ?? t?._seconds;
    if(Number.isFinite(Number(sec))){
      const nano=Number(t?.nanoseconds ?? t?._nanoseconds ?? 0);
      return Number(sec)*1000 + Math.floor(nano/1e6);
    }
    if(t instanceof Date){const v=t.getTime();if(Number.isFinite(v))return v;}
    if(typeof t==='number'&&Number.isFinite(t)) return t;
    if(typeof t==='string'){

};
function muralKindMeta(kind){return MURAL_KINDS[kind]||MURAL_KINDS.atualizacao}
function muralMatches(item){
  const filter=muralFilter||'all';
  if(filter==='all') return true;
  if(filter==='recado') return item.__type==='recado';
  if(item.__type!=='post') return false;
  const k=item.kind||'atualizacao';
  if(filter==='ajuda') return k.startsWith('ajuda_');
  return k===filter;
}
function muralTime(item){
  // Aceita Timestamp real do Firestore, objetos serializados e campos antigos.
  const candidates=[item?.ts,item?.createdAt,item?.updatedAt,item?.timestamp,item?.date];
  for(const t of candidates){
    if(!t) continue;
    if(typeof t?.toMillis==='function'){
      const v=t.toMillis(); if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0) return v;
    }
    const sec=t?.seconds ?? t?._seconds;
    if(Number.isFinite(Number(sec))){
      const nano=Number(t?.nanoseconds ?? t?._nanoseconds ?? 0);
      return Number(sec)*1000 + Math.floor(nano/1e6);
    }
    if(t instanceof Date){const v=t.getTime();if(Number.isFinite(v))return v;}
    if(typeof t==='number'&&Number.isFinite(t)) return t;
    if(typeof t==='string'){
      const v=Date.parse(t); if(Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}

let muralFilter='all';
const MURAL_KINDS={
  ajuda_campo:{icon:'🌿',label:'Ajuda pra campo',cls:'ajuda_campo'},

  ideia:{icon:'💡',label:'Ideia / sugestão',cls:'ideia'},

};
function muralKindMeta(kind){return MURAL_KINDS[kind]||MURAL_KINDS.atualizacao}
function muralMatches(item){
  const filter=muralFilter||'all';
  if(filter==='all') return true;
  if(filter==='recado') return item.__type==='recado';
  if(item.__type!=='post') return false;
  const k=item.kind||'atualizacao';
  if(filter==='ajuda') return k.startsWith('ajuda_');
  return k===filter;
}
function muralTime(item){
  // Aceita Timestamp real do Firestore, objetos serializados e campos antigos.
  const candidates=[item?.ts,item?.createdAt,item?.updatedAt,item?.timestamp,item?.date];
  for(const t of candidates){
    if(!t) continue;
    if(typeof t?.toMillis==='function'){
      const v=t.toMillis(); if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0) return v;
    }
    const sec=t?.seconds ?? t?._seconds;
    if(Number.isFinite(Number(sec))){
      const nano=Number(t?.nanoseconds ?? t?._nanoseconds ?? 0);
      return Number(sec)*1000 + Math.floor(nano/1e6);
    }
    if(t instanceof Date){const v=t.getTime();if(Number.isFinite(v))return v;}
    if(typeof t==='number'&&Number.isFinite(t)) return t;
    if(typeof t==='string'){
      const v=Date.parse(t); if(Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}
function muralItemHTML(item){
  const ms=item.__ms||muralTime(item)||Date.now();
  if(item.__type==='post'){
    const m=memberForAuthor(item.authorId,item.authorName),can=is

alLoadedKey=mk;medalLoadedPoints=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));renderMedalsFrom(medalLoadedPoints)}catch(e){console.error(e);$('#medalBoard').innerHTML='<div class="ag-empty">Não foi possível carregar o ranking.</div>'}}

function markTopPlus(){
  if(innerWidth>760)return;
  const add=$('#addBtn');
  if(add){add.classList.remove('p103-top-plus');add.title='Adicionar aluno';add.setAttribute('aria-label','Adicionar aluno')}
  $$('button').forEach(b=>{
    if(b===add)return;
    if(String(b.textContent||'').trim()!=='+')return;
    if(b.closest('.private-composer,.p46-compose,.p48-compose-row,#softBottomNav'))return;
    const r=b.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(r.top>=0&&r.top<360&&r.width<=120&&r.height<=120)b.classList.add('p103-top-plus');
  })
}

}

/* Carbonautas P128 · menos coisas na tela
   1) Medalhas e pontos saem da interface (os dados continuam no Firestore).
   2) Check-in com texto sai. Entrar no app já registra presença (rede_members.lastSeenAt).
   3) Coordenação vê no Painel quem entrou e quando. Só a coordenação.
   4) Linha do tempo por pessoa dentro de Pessoas (prazos, tarefas, arquivos, fotos, destaques).
   5) O jogo só carrega quando a tela do jogo é aberta. */

function me(){try{return typeof myId!=='undefined'?myId:''}catch(_e){return''}}
function admin(){try{return !!isAdmin}catch(_e){return false}}
function st(){try{return state||{}}catch(_e){return{}}}
function ms(v){if(!v)return 0;try{if(typeof v.toMillis==='function')return v.toMillis();if(v.seconds)return v.seconds*1000;if(typeof v==='string'){if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v))return new Date(v+'T12:00:00').getTime();const t=Date.parse(v);return isNaN(t)?0:t}if(typeof v==='number')return v;if(v instanceof Date)return v.getTime()}catch(_e){}return 0}
function dayIso(d){const x=d||new Date();return x.getFullYear()+'-'+String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(x.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}
function fmtShort(t){if(!t)return'';return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})}
function ago(t){if(!t)return'nunca';const d=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/86400000);if(d<=0)return'hoje';if(d===1)return'ontem';if(d<30)return`${d} d`;const m=Math.floor(d/30);return m<12?`${m} m`:`${Math.floor(m/12)} a`}
function first(n){return String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/)[0]||'Carbonauta'}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function ago(t){if(!t)return'nunca';const d=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/86400000);if(d<=0)return'hoje';if(d===1)return'ontem';if(d<30)return`${d} d`;const m=Math.floor(d/30);return m<12?`${m} m`:`${Math.floor(m/12)} a`}
function first(n){return String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/)[0]||'Carbonauta'}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 4) linha do tempo por pessoa ---------- */
const SHOW=12;
function timelineItems(m){
  const items=[];
  (m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.data);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:p.feito?'done':(t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`Prazo · ${p.titulo||''}`})});
  (st().activities||[]).filter(a=>a.ownerId===m.id).forEach(a=>{
    const t=ms(a.dueDate)||ms(a.updatedAt)||ms(a.createdAt);if(!t)return;
    const done=a.status==='concluido'||!!a.doneAt;
    items.push({t,cls:done?'done':(a.dueDate&&t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`${a.type==='produto'?'Produto':a.type==='orientacao'?'Orientação':'Tarefa'} · ${a.title||''}`})
  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Arquivo · ${p.titulo||p.fileName||''}${p.reviewFlow?' · v'+(p.reviewVersion||1):''}`})});
  (st().photos||[]).filter(p=>p.autorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.dataRealizacao)||ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Foto · ${p.legenda||p.projeto||''}`})});
  (st().feed||[]).filter(p=>p.authorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Destaque · ${p.title||p.text||''}`})});
  return items.sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
}
function renderTimeline(expand){
  const inner=document.getElementById('detailInner');if(!inner)return;
  let m=null;try{m=(st().members||[]).find(x=>x.id===selectedId)}catch(_e){}
  if(!m)return;
  const items=timelineItems(m);
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Timeline');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('div');box.id='p128Timeline';inner.appendChild(box)}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 4) linha do tempo por pessoa ---------- */
const SHOW=12;
function timelineItems(m){
  const items=[];
  (m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.data);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:p.feito?'done':(t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`Prazo · ${p.titulo||''}`})});
  (st().activities||[]).filter(a=>a.ownerId===m.id).forEach(a=>{
    const t=ms(a.dueDate)||ms(a.updatedAt)||ms(a.createdAt);if(!t)return;
    const done=a.status==='concluido'||!!a.doneAt;
    items.push({t,cls:done?'done':(a.dueDate&&t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`${a.type==='produto'?'Produto':a.type==='orientacao'?'Orientação':'Tarefa'} · ${a.title||''}`})
  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 4) linha do tempo por pessoa ---------- */
const SHOW=12;
function timelineItems(m){
  const items=[];
  (m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.data);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:p.feito?'done':(t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`Prazo · ${p.titulo||''}`})});
  (st().activities||[]).filter(a=>a.ownerId===m.id).forEach(a=>{
    const t=ms(a.dueDate)||ms(a.updatedAt)||ms(a.createdAt);if(!t)return;
    const done=a.status==='concluido'||!!a.doneAt;
    items.push({t,cls:done?'done':(a.dueDate&&t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`${a.type==='produto'?'Produto':a.type==='orientacao'?'Orientação':'Tarefa'} · ${a.title||''}`})
  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt

function ensureCss(){if(document.getElementById('p128Style'))return;const s=document.createElement('style');s.id='p128Style';s.textContent=css;document.head.appendChild(s)}

/* ---------- utilidades seguras ---------- */
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
function me(){try{return typeof myId!=='undefined'?myId:''}catch(_e){return''}}
function admin(){try{return !!isAdmin}catch(_e){return false}}
function st(){try{return state||{}}catch(_e){return{}}}
function ms(v){if(!v)return 0;try{if(typeof v.toMillis==='function')return v.toMillis();if(v.seconds)return v.seconds*1000;if(typeof v==='string'){if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v))return new Date(v+'T12:00:00').getTime();const t=Date.parse(v);return isNaN(t)?0:t}if(typeof v==='number')return v;if(v instanceof Date)return v.getTime()}catch(_e){}return 0}
function dayIso(d){const x=d||new Date();return x.getFullYear()+'-'+String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(x.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}
function fmtShort(t){if(!t)return'';return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})}
function ago(t){if(!t)return'nunca';const d=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/86400000);if(d<=0)return'hoje';if(d===1)return'ontem';if(d<30)return`${d} d`;const m=Math.floor(d/30);return m<12?`${m} m`:`${Math.floor(m/12)} a`}
function first(n){return String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/)[0]||'Carbonauta'}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');

  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function ago(t){if(!t)return'nunca';const d=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/86400000);if(d<=0)return'hoje';if(d===1)return'ontem';if(d<30)return`${d} d`;const m=Math.floor(d/30);return m<12?`${m} m`:`${Math.floor(m/12)} a`}
function first(n){return String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/)[0]||'Carbonauta'}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 4) linha do tempo por pessoa ---------- */
const SHOW=12;
function timelineItems(m){
  const items=[];
  (m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.data);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:p.feito?'done':(t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`Prazo · ${p.titulo||''}`})});
  (st().activities||[]).filter(a=>a.ownerId===m.id).forEach(a=>{
    const t=ms(a.dueDate)||ms(a.updatedAt)||ms(a.createdAt);if(!t)return;
    const done=a.status==='concluido'||!!a.doneAt;
    items.push({t,cls:done?'done':(a.dueDate&&t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`${a.type==='produto'?'Produto':a.type==='orientacao'?'Orientação':'Tarefa'} · ${a.title||''}`})
  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Arquivo · ${p.titulo||p.fileName||''}${p.reviewFlow?' · v'+(p.reviewVersion||1):''}`})});
  (st().photos||[]).filter(p=>p.autorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.dataRealizacao)||ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Foto · ${p.legenda||p.projeto||''}`})});
  (st().feed||[]).filter(p=>p.authorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Destaque · ${p.title||p.text||''}`})});
  return items.sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
}

  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Arquivo · ${p.titulo||p.fileName||''}${p.reviewFlow?' · v'+(p.reviewVersion||1):''}`})});

function renderTimeline(expand){
  const inner=document.getElementById('detailInner');if(!inner)return;
  let m=null;try{m=(st().members||[]).find(x=>x.id===selectedId)}catch(_e){}
  if(!m)return;
  const items=timelineItems(m);
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Timeline');

function renderTimeline(expand){
  const inner=document.getElementById('detailInner');if(!inner)return;
  let m=null;try{m=(st().members||[]).find(x=>x.id===selectedId)}catch(_e){}
  if(!m)return;
  const items=timelineItems(m);
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Timeline');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('div');box.id='p128Timeline';inner.appendChild(box)}
  const all=expand||box.dataset.expand==='1';box.dataset.expand=all?'1':'0';
  const shown=all?items:items.slice(0,SHOW);
  box.innerHTML=`<h4>Linha do tempo</h4>`+(items.length?`<ol>${shown.map(i=>`<li class="${i.cls}"><time>${fmtShort(i.t)}</time>${esc(i.txt).slice(0,90)}</li>`).join('')}</ol>${items.length>SHOW&&!all?`<button type="button" class="p128-more">Ver tudo (${items.length})</button>`:''}`:`<div class="p128-empty">Nada registrado ainda.</div>`);
  const more=box.querySelector('.p128-more');if(more)more.onclick=()=>renderTimeline(true);
}

/* ---------- 5) jogo só quando aberto ---------- */
function loadGameIfOpen(v){
  if(v!=='jogo')return;
  const f=document.getElementById('carbonautaGameFrame');
  if(f&&!f.getAttribute('src')&&f.dataset.src)f.src=f.dataset.src;
}

/* ---------- ganchos ---------- */
let basePainel=null,baseDetail=null,baseSwitch=null;
function hook(){
  if(!basePainel&&typeof window.renderPainel==='function'){basePainel=window.renderPainel;const w=function(){const r=basePainel.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(renderPresence,0);return r};try{renderPainel=w}catch(_e){}window.renderPainel=w}
  if(!baseDetail&&typeof window.renderDetail==='function'){baseDetail=window.renderDetail;const w=function(){const r=baseDetail.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(()=>renderTimeline(false),0);return r};try{renderDetail=w}catch(_e){}window.renderDetail=w}
  if(!baseSwitch&&typeof window.switchView==='function'){baseSwitch=window.switchView;const w=function(v){const r=baseSwitch.apply(this,arguments);loadGameIfOpen(v);return r};try{switchView=w}catch(_e){}window.switchView=w}
}
function boot(){
  ensureCss();hook();
  setTimeout(hook,900);setTimeout(hook,2500);
  const tick=setInterval(()=>{hook();if(me()){markPresence();renderPresence();}if(presenceDone&&basePainel&&baseDetail)clearInterval(tick)},1500);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden){presenceDone=false;markPresence()}});
  console.info('Carbonautas',BUILD,'presença silenciosa, linha do tempo, sem medalhas, jogo sob demanda');
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>

function startAuth(){FB().onAuthStateChanged(window.auth,user=>{handleAuthUser(user).catch(e=>{console.error('Auth observer:',e);showLogin('Não foi possível abrir a sessão. Tente novamente.')})})}

function refreshIdentityUI(){
  const chip=$('#idChip'),av=$('#idAv'),nm=$('#idName'),me=resolveCurrentMember();
  const displayName=me?.nome||authProfile?.nome||authUser?.email||'Entrar';
  nm.textContent=displayName;
  av.innerHTML=me?.foto?`<img src="${esc(me.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(displayName);
  av.style.background=me?(NIVEIS[me.nivel]?.color||'var(--ink-3)'):'var(--ink-3)';
  chip.classList.toggle('admin',isAdmin);
  if($('#whoName')) $('#whoName').textContent=displayName;
  if($('#whoDot')) $('#whoDot').style.background=me?(NIVEIS[me.nivel]?.color||'var(--ink-3)'):'var(--ink-3)';
  $('#adminBtn').style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  $('#adminBtn').title='Gerenciar acessos';
  $('#lockIcon').innerHTML=isAdmin?'<path d="M12 3 4 7v5c0 5 3.4 8.5 8 9 4.6-.5 8-4 8-9V7z"/><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"/>':'';
  updateAdminButtons();
}
function updateAdminButtons(){
  const add=$('#addBtn'); if(add){add.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none'; add.title=isAdmin?'Adicionar aluno à rede':'Somente coordenador';}
  const ev=$('#newEventBtn'); if(ev)ev.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  const ml=$('#manageLinhasBtn'); if(ml)ml.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  const rc=$('#recadoBtn'); if(rc)rc.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
  const pb=$('#peopleAdminBlock'); if(pb)pb.style.display=isAdmin?'block':'none';
  const mp=$('#managePeopleBtn'); if(mp)mp.style.display=isAdmin?'inline-flex':'none';
}

  if(isAdmin) populateAccessMemberSelects();
}

}
function populateAccessMemberSelects(){
  const linked=new Set((state.users||[]).map(u=>u.memberId)), opts=[...state.members].sort((a,b)=>a.nome.localeCompare(b.nome)).map(m=>`<option value="${m.id}" ${linked.has(m.id)?'disabled':''}>${esc(m.nome)}${linked.has(m.id)?' — já vinculado':''}</option>`).join('');
  ['accessMember','linkMember'].forEach(id=>{const el=$('#'+id);if(el)el.innerHTML=opts});
}

function renderAccessList(){
  const el=$('#accessList');if(!el)return; if(!isAdmin){el.innerHTML='';return}
  const rows=[...(state.users||[])].sort((a,b)=>(a.nome||'').localeCompare(b.nome||''));
  el.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(u=>{const m=memberById(u.memberId),self=u.uid===authUser?.uid,broken=!m;return `<div class="access-row"><div class="main"><div class="nm">${esc(m?.nome||u.nome||u.memberId||'Sem perfil')} ${u.role==='coordinator'?'<span class="role-pill">coordenador</span>':''} ${self?'<span class="role-pill">conta atual</span>':''} ${broken?'<span class="role-pill" style="background:#fff0f0;color:#c62828">vínculo quebrado</span>':''}</div><div class="em">${esc(u.email||'')} · ${esc(u.uid)}</div></div>${self?'':`<button class="btn danger" onclick="unlinkAccess('${u.uid}')">Desvincular</button>`}</div>`}).join(''):'<div class="hint">Nenhuma conta vinculada.</div>';
  populateAccessMemberSelects();
}
async function createAccess(){
  if(!isAdmin)return; const memberId=$('#accessMember').value,email=$('#accessEmail').value.trim(),password=$('#accessPassword').value; const m=memberById(memberId);
  if(!memberId||!email||password.length<6){toast('Selecione a pessoa, informe e-mail e senha com 6+ caracteres.');return}
  $('#createAccessBtn').disabled=true; let created=null;
  try{
    const cred=await FB().createUserWithEmailAndPassword(window.secondaryAuth,email,password); created=cred.user;
    await FB().signOut(window.secondaryAuth);
    await FB().setDoc(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',created.uid),{memberId,nome:m?.nome||'',email,role:'student',createdAt:FB().serverTimestamp(),createdBy:authUser.uid});
    $('#accessEmail').value='';$('#accessPassword').value='';toast('Conta criada e vinculada a '+(m?.nome||'Carbonauta')+'.');
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast(created?`A conta foi criada (UID ${created.uid}), mas o vínculo falhou. Vincule o UID abaixo.`:authErrorMessage(e));}
  finally{$('#createAccessBtn').disabled=false}
}

async function unlinkAccess(uidv){if(!isAdmin||uidv===authUser?.uid||!confirm('Desvincular esta conta da plataforma?'))return;try{const u=(state.users||[]).find(x=>x.uid===uidv);const batch=FB().writeBatch(window.db);batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uidv));if(u?.memberId)batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',u.memberId));await batch.commit();toast('Acesso desvinculado. O perfil poderá ser reivindicado novamente.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Erro ao desvincular.')}}

/* ============ FILTER LOGIC ============ */
function visibleMembers(){
  const q=$('#search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const prog=$('#fPrograma').value, st=$('#fStatus').value;
  return state.members.filter(m=>{
    if(hiddenNiveis.has(m.nivel)) return false;
    if(prog && m.programa!==prog) return false;
    if(st && m.status!==st) return false;
    if(hiddenLinhas.size){ if(!m.linhas.some(l=>!hiddenLinhas.has(l))) return false; }
    if(q){
      const hay=(m.nome+' '+m.tema+' '+m.linhas.join(' ')+' '+m.programa+' '+(m.origem||'')).toLowerCase();
      if(!hay.includes(q)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}
/* reasons: [{type:'linha'|'hub'|'vinculo', label}] entre dois membros específicos.
   'linha' respeita o filtro hiddenLinhas; 'hub' e 'vinculo' não (não são linhas de pesquisa). */
function pairReasons(a,b,includeAux){
  const reasons=[];
  a.linhas.filter(l=>b.linhas.includes(l) && !hiddenLinhas.has(l))
    .forEach(l=>reasons.push({type:'linha',label:l}));
  if(includeAux){
    if(a.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:a.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    if(b.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:b.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    (a.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===b.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
    (b.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===a.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
  }
  return reasons;
}
function p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,mode){
  if(!mode||mode==='all')return true;
  const txt=String(r?.label||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
  if(mode==='orientacao')return /orienta|orientad|coorient|supervis/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='projetos')return r?.type==='linha'||/projeto|pesquisa|campo|grupo|rede/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='producao')return /artigo|publica|produc|coautor|manuscrit|relat|dado|dataset|capitul|livro/.test(txt);
  return true;
}

  const includeAux = $('#tHubLinks') ? $('#tHubLinks').checked : true;

  let fotoUrl=formFoto;
  if(formFoto && formFoto.startsWith('data:')){
    try{
      toast('Enviando foto…');
      const {url}=await fbUploadImage('membro_'+(editingId||nome.replace(/\s+/g,'_')),formFoto);
      fotoUrl=url;
    }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Falha ao enviar a foto. Salvando sem imagem.'); fotoUrl=''; }
  }

  const data={
    nome, nivel:$('#mNivel').value, programa:$('#mPrograma').value.trim(), status:$('#mStatus').value,
    bolsista:$('#mBolsista').checked, bolsaModalidade:$('#mBolsaModalidade').value.trim(), bolsaInicio:$('#mBolsaInicio').value, bolsaFim:$('#mBolsaFim').value,
    origem:$('#mOrigem').value.trim(), telefone:$('#mTelefone').value.trim(),
    tema:$('#mTema').value.trim(), plataforma:$('#mPlataforma').value.trim(), driveLink:$('#mDrive').value.trim(), notas:$('#mNotas').value.trim(),
    linhas:[...formLinhas], resultados:formResultados.filter(r=>r.titulo||r.url), prazos:formPrazos.filter(p=>p.titulo), foto:fotoUrl,
    hub:$('#mHub').checked, hubMotivo:$('#mHubMotivo').value.trim(),
    vinculos:formVinculos.filter(v=>v.id)
  };

    if(editingId){ await fbSetMember(editingId,data); }
    else { const id=uid(); await fbSetMember(id,{id,...data,createdAt:FB().serverTimestamp()}); }
    closeOverlay('memberOverlay'); toast(editingId?'Aluno atualizado.':'Aluno cadastrado.');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Erro ao salvar. Verifique a conexão.'); }
}
async function deleteMember(){
  if(!editingId || !isAdmin)return;
  const m=memberById(editingId);
  if(m && m.protegido){ toast('O perfil do coordenador não pode ser removido por aqui.'); return; }
  if(!confirm('Excluir este aluno da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso serão removidos. Esta ação não pode ser desfeita.'))return;
  try{
    const idToDelete=editingId, f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===idToDelete);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db); batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',idToDelete)); batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',idToDelete)); linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid))); await batch.commit();
    closeOverlay('memberOverlay'); if(selectedId===idToDelete)closeDetail();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede e acesso desvinculado.');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Erro ao remover.'); }
}

function renderPeopleManager(){
  if(!isAdmin || !$('#peopleList')) return;
  const q=($('#peopleSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const items=[...state.members].filter(m=>!q || [m.nome,m.programa,m.status,m.origem].some(v=>(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>(a.nome||'').localeCompare(b.nome||''));
  $('#peopleList').innerHTML=items.map(m=>{const nv=NIVEIS[m.nivel]||{color:'#607d8b',short:m.nivel||''};return `<div class="people-row"><div class="p-av" style="background:${nv.color}">${m.foto?`<img src="${esc(m.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(m.nome)}</div><div><div class="p-name">${esc(m.nome)}</div><div class="p-meta">${esc(nv.short)}${m.programa?' · '+esc(m.programa):''}${m.status==='egresso'?' · Egresso':''}${m.bolsista?' · 🎓 Bolsista':''}</div></div><div class="p-actions"><button type="button" class="btn" onclick="editPersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Editar / vínculos</button>${!m.protegido?`<button type="button" class="btn danger" onclick="removePersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Excluir aluno</button>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('') || '<div class="crono-empty">Nenhuma pessoa encontrada.</div>';
}

function renderPeopleManager(){
  if(!isAdmin || !$('#peopleList')) return;
  const q=($('#peopleSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const items=[...state.members].filter(m=>!q || [m.nome,m.programa,m.status,m.origem].some(v=>(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>(a.nome||'').localeCompare(b.nome||''));
  $('#peopleList').innerHTML=items.map(m=>{const nv=NIVEIS[m.nivel]||{color:'#607d8b',short:m.nivel||''};return `<div class="people-row"><div class="p-av" style="background:${nv.color}">${m.foto?`<img src="${esc(m.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(m.nome)}</div><div><div class="p-name">${esc(m.nome)}</div><div class="p-meta">${esc(nv.short)}${m.programa?' · '+esc(m.programa):''}${m.status==='egresso'?' · Egresso':''}${m.bolsista?' · 🎓 Bolsista':''}</div></div><div class="p-actions"><button type="button" class="btn" onclick="editPersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Editar / vínculos</button>${!m.protegido?`<button type="button" class="btn danger" onclick="removePersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Excluir aluno</button>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('') || '<div class="crono-empty">Nenhuma pessoa encontrada.</div>';
}
function openPeopleManager(){if(!isAdmin)return;renderPeopleManager();openOverlay('peopleOverlay')}
function editPersonFromManager(id){closeOverlay('peopleOverlay');openMember(id)}
async function removePersonFromManager(id){
  if(!isAdmin)return; const m=memberById(id); if(!m||m.protegido)return;
  if(!confirm(`Excluir ${m.nome} da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso também serão removidos.`))return;
  try{
    const f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===id);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db);
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id));
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',id));
    linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid)));
    await batch.commit();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede.'); renderPeopleManager();
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'Permissão negada pelas regras do Firestore.':'Erro ao retirar pessoa.')}
}

  const q=($('#peopleSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const items=[...state.members].filter(m=>!q || [m.nome,m.programa,m.status,m.origem].some(v=>(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>(a.nome||'').localeCompare(b.nome||''));
  $('#peopleList').innerHTML=items.map(m=>{const nv=NIVEIS[m.nivel]||{color:'#607d8b',short:m.nivel||''};return `<div class="people-row"><div class="p-av" style="background:${nv.color}">${m.foto?`<img src="${esc(m.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(m.nome)}</div><div><div class="p-name">${esc(m.nome)}</div><div class="p-meta">${esc(nv.short)}${m.programa?' · '+esc(m.programa):''}${m.status==='egresso'?' · Egresso':''}${m.bolsista?' · 🎓 Bolsista':''}</div></div><div class="p-actions"><button type="button" class="btn" onclick="editPersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Editar / vínculos</button>${!m.protegido?`<button type="button" class="btn danger" onclick="removePersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Excluir aluno</button>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('') || '<div class="crono-empty">Nenhuma pessoa encontrada.</div>';
}
function openPeopleManager(){if(!isAdmin)return;renderPeopleManager();openOverlay('peopleOverlay')}
function editPersonFromManager(id){closeOverlay('peopleOverlay');openMember(id)}
async function removePersonFromManager(id){
  if(!isAdmin)return; const m=memberById(id); if(!m||m.protegido)return;
  if(!confirm(`Excluir ${m.nome} da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso também serão removidos.`))return;
  try{
    const f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===id);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db);
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id));
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',id));
    linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid)));
    await batch.commit();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede.'); renderPeopleManager();
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'Permissão negada pelas regras do Firestore.':'Erro ao retirar pessoa.')}
}

let previewObjectUrl='', previewWorkbook=null;
function inferFileExt(url,name=''){
  const n=(name||'').split('?')[0].toLowerCase(); const m=n.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,6})$/); if(m)return m[1];
  try{const d=decodeURIComponent(new URL(url).pathname).toLowerCase();const x=d.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,6})$/);return x?x[1]:''}catch(e){return ''}
}
function sanitizePreviewHtml(html){
  const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+html+'</div>','text/html');
  doc.querySelectorAll('script,style,iframe,object,embed,form').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  doc.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(el=>{
    [...el.attributes].forEach(a=>{const n=a.name.toLowerCase(),v=(a.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(n.startsWith('on')||(n==='href'&&v.startsWith('javascript:')))el.removeAttribute(a.name)});
  });
  return doc.body.firstElementChild?.innerHTML||'';
}
async function previewFetch(url){
  const r=await fetch(url,{credentials:'omit',cache:'no-store'});
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error('Não foi possível carregar o arquivo ('+r.status+').');
  return r.arrayBuffer();
}
function onlineViewerHtml(url,name='',service='google'){
  const safeUrl=encodeURIComponent(url), safeName=esc(name||'Arquivo');
  const src=service==='office'
    ? `https://view.officeapps.live.com/op/embed.aspx?src=${safeUrl}`
    : `https://docs.google.com/gview?embedded=1&url=${safeUrl}`;
  return `<div class="online-preview-wrap"><div class="online-preview-note"><span>Visualização online de <b>${safeName}</b></span><div><button class="btn" type="button" onclick="switchOnlineViewer('google')">Google</button><button class="btn" type="button" onclick="switchOnlineViewer('office')">Microsoft</button></div></div><iframe class="file-preview-frame" src="${src}" title="${safeName}" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"></i
---
();
}

function _repoNorm(v=''){return String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ')}
function _repoTsMillis(p){try{return p?.ts?.toMillis?p.ts.toMillis():(p?.ts?.seconds?Number(p.ts.seconds)*1000:+new Date(p?.ts||0)||0)}catch(_e){return 0}}
function repoReviewThreadId(p){return p?.reviewThreadId||p?.id||''}
function repoExactDuplicateKey(p){
  if(!p)return '';
  if(p.reviewFlow&&p.url&&p.reviewVersion)return `review|${repoReviewThreadId(p)}|${Number(p.reviewVersion)}|${p.url}`;
  if(p.sourcePersonalRepo&&p.sourcePackageId&&p.sourcePackageFileId)return `personal|${p.sourcePackageId}|${p.sourcePackageFileId}`;
  if(p.url&&p.fileName)return `file|${p.memberId||''}|${_repoNorm(p.titulo)}|${_repoNorm(p.fileName)}|${p.url}`;
  return '';
}
function compactPublicationsForDisplay(list){
  const out=[],seenThreads=new Set(),seenExact=new Set();
  [...(list||[])].sort((a,b)=>_repoTsMillis(b)-_repoTsMillis(a)).forEach(p=>{
    if(p.reviewFlow){const tid=repoReviewThreadId(p);if(seenThreads.has(tid))return;seenThreads.add(tid);out.push(p);return;}
    const k=repoExactDuplicateKey(p);if(k&&seenExact.has(k))return;if(k)seenExact.add(k);out.push(p);
  });
  return out;
}

function compactPublicationsForDisplay(list){
  const out=[],seenThreads=new Set(),seenExact=new Set();
  [...(list||[])].sort((a,b)=>_repoTsMillis(b)-_repoTsMillis(a)).forEach(p=>{
    if(p.reviewFlow){const tid=repoReviewThreadId(p);if(seenThreads.has(tid))return;seenThreads.add(tid);out.push(p);return;}

function repoExactDuplicateGroups(){
  const groups=new Map();
  (state.publicacoes||[]).forEach(p=>{const k=repoExactDuplicateKey(p);if(!k)return;if(!groups.has(k))groups.set(k,[]);groups.get(k).push(p)});
  return [...groups.values()].filter(g=>g.length>1).map(g=>g.sort((a,b)=>_repoTsMillis(b)-_repoTsMillis(a)));
}

function _repoNorm(v=''){return String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ')}
function _repoTsMillis(p){try{return p?.ts?.toMillis?p.ts.toMillis():(p?.ts?.seconds?Number(p.ts.seconds)*1000:+new Date(p?.ts||0)||0)}catch(_e){return 0}}
function repoReviewThreadId(p){return p?.reviewThreadId||p?.id||''}
function repoExactDuplicateKey(p){
  if(!p)return '';
  if(p.reviewFlow&&p.url&&p.reviewVersion)return `review|${repoReviewThreadId(p)}|${Number(p.reviewVersion)}|${p.url}`;
  if(p.sourcePersonalRepo&&p.sourcePackageId&&p.sourcePackageFileId)return `personal|${p.sourcePackageId}|${p.sourcePackageFileId}`;
  if(p.url&&p.fileName)return `file|${p.memberId||''}|${_repoNorm(p.titulo)}|${_repoNorm(p.fileName)}|${p.url}`;
  return '';
}
function compactPublicationsForDisplay(list){
  const out=[],seenThreads=new Set(),seenExact=new Set();
  [...(list||[])].sort((a,b)=>_repoTsMillis(b)-_repoTsMillis(a)).forEach(p=>{
    if(p.reviewFlow){const tid=repoReviewThreadId(p);if(seenThreads.has(tid))return;seenThreads.add(tid);out.push(p);return;}
    const k=repoExactDuplicateKey(p);if(k&&seenExact.has(k))return;if(k)seenExact.add(k);out.push(p);
  });
  return out;
}
function repoExactDuplicateGroups(){
  const groups=new Map();
  (state.publicacoes||[]).forEach(p=>{const k=repoExactDuplicateKey(p);if(!k)return;if(!groups.has(k))groups.set(k,[]);groups.get(k).push(p)});
  return [...groups.values()].filter(g=>g.length>1).map(g=>g.sort((a,b)=>_repoTsMillis(b)-_repoTsMillis(a)));
}

function repoFileIcon(name=''){const e=inferFileExt('',name);if(e==='pdf')return'📕';if(['doc','docx','rtf','odt'].includes(e))return'📄';if(['xls','xlsx','csv'].includes(e))return'📊';if(['ppt','pptx'].includes(e))return'📽️';if(['png','jpg','jpeg','webp','gif','svg'].includes(e))return'🖼️';return'📎'}
function humanBytes(n=0){n=Number(n)||0;if(n<1024)return n+' B';if(n<1048576)return(n/1024).toFixed(1)+' KB';return(n/1048576).toFixed(1)+' MB'}
async function openRepositoryPackage(id){

function rootAllowedMemberIds(ownerId){
  const root=repoRootByOwner(ownerId);
  const ids=new Set([ownerId,...((root?.collaboratorMemberIds||root?.allowedMemberIds||[]).filter(Boolean))]);
  return ids;
}
function fileToDataUrl(file){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>res(r.result);r.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('Falha ao ler o arquivo.'));r.readAsDataURL(file)})}
function renderRepoPackagePeople(){
  const wrap=$('#repoPackagePeople');if(!wrap)return;
  const q=($('#repoPackagePeopleSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const ownerId=(repoPackageUploadContext==='personal'||!isAdmin)?myId:($('#repoPackageOwner')?.value||myId);
  const root=rootAllowedMemberIds(ownerId);
  const checked=new Set([...wrap.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked')].map(x=>x.value));
  wrap.innerHTML=state.members.filter(m=>m.status!=='inativo'&&m.id!==ownerId&&(!q||m.nome.toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>a.nome.localeCompare(b.nome,'pt-BR')).map(m=>{
    const viaRoot=root.has(m.id)&&m.id!==ownerId;
    return `<label class="repo-share-row"><input type="checkbox" value="${m.id}" ${checked.has(m.id)||viaRoot?'checked':''} ${viaRoot?'disabled':''}><span><b>${esc(m.nome)}</b><small>${viaRoot?'Já tem acesso ao repositório inteiro':esc(NIVEIS[m.nivel]?.label||m.programa||'Integrante da Rede')}</small></span></label>`;
  }).join('')||'<div class="hint">Nenhuma pessoa encontrada.</div>';
}

function p56SetAck(key,meta={}){
  const all=p56LoadAcks();all[key]={...meta,key,doneAt:Date.now(),doneBy:(typeof myName!=='undefined'&&myName)||memberById?.(myId)?.nome||'Você'};p56SaveAcks(all);return all[key];
}
function p56UnsetAck(key){const all=p56LoadAcks();delete all[key];p56SaveAcks(all)}
function p56IsAck(key){return !!p56LoadAcks()[key]}

function p56ActivityLabel(a){
  const type=(typeof ACTIVITY_TYPES!=='undefined'&&ACTIVITY_TYPES[a?.type])||a?.type||'Acompanhamento';
  return a?.title||type;

function p56ActivityKind(a){
  if(a?.type==='correcao')return 'CORREÇÃO · ACOMPANHAMENTO';
  if(a?.type==='pendencia')return 'PENDÊNCIA · ACOMPANHAMENTO';
  if(a?.type==='produto')return 'PRODUTO · ACOMPANHAMENTO';
  return 'PRAZO · ACOMPANHAMENTO';
}
function p56BuildItems(){
  const scope=p56Scope(),ids=new Set(scope.map(m=>m.id)),open=[],done=[];
  const mk=monthKey();
  const acks=p56LoadAcks();

  // Ações reais do acompanhamento: uma linha por tarefa, nunca resumo genérico.
  (state.activities||[]).filter(a=>ids.has(a.ownerId)).forEach(a=>{
    const isDone=_activityDone(a),di=a.dueDate?dateInfo(a.dueDate):null;
    const isCorr=a.type==='correcao';
    const relevant=!isDone && (isCorr || (di&&(di.over||(di.diff>=0&&di.diff<=7))));
    const updated=p56Millis(a.updatedAt)||p56Millis(a.createdAt);
    if(relevant){
      const sev=di?.over||((di?.diff??99)<=2)?'critical':'warn';
      let detail=isCorr&&!a.dueDate?'Correção aberta.':di?.over?`Venceu em ${fmtDate(a.dueDate)}.`:di?`Vence ${di.diff===0?'hoje':`em ${di.diff} dia(s)`} · ${fmtDate(a.dueDate)}.`:'Requer acompanhamento.';
      open.push({key:`activity:${a.id}`,kind:p56ActivityKind(a),who:p56MemberName(a.ownerId),what:p56ActivityLabel(a),detail,date:a.dueDate||'',sev,sort:sev==='critical'?0:3,action:'activity',sourceId:a.id,memberId:a.ownerId,canComplete:true});
    }
    if(isDone && p56MonthFromMillis(updated)===mk){
      done.push({key:`doneactivity:${a.id}`,kind:`✓ ${(typeof ACTIVITY_TYPES!=='undefined'&&ACTIVITY_TYPES[a.type])||a.type||'Acompanhamento'}`,who:p56MemberName(a.ownerId),what:p56ActivityLabel(a),doneAt:updated,sourceId:a.id,memberId:a.ownerId,backendDone:true});
    }
  });

  // Correções do Repositório em que o usuário logado realmente precisa agir.
  try{
    const byThread=new Map();
    (state.publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.reviewFlow&&typeof canActOnReview==='function'&&canActOnReview(p)).forEach(p=>{
      const tid=(typeof repoReviewThreadId==='function'&&repoReviewThreadId(p))||p.reviewThreadId||p.id;
      const old=byThread.get(tid);if(!old||Number(p.reviewVersion||1)>Number(old.reviewVersion||1))byThread.set(tid,p);
    });
    byThread.forEach((p,tid)=>{
      const key=`review:${tid}`;if(p56IsAck(key))return;
      const act=(typeof reviewActionLabel==='function'?reviewActionLabel(p):'Revisar arquivo');
      open.push({key,kind:'CORREÇÃO · REPOSITÓRIO',who:p.memberNome||p.reviewSenderName||p56MemberName(p.memberId),what:p.reviewBaseTitle||p.titulo||p.fileName||'Arquivo para correção',detail:`${act} · versão ${Number(p.reviewVersion||1)}.`,date:'',sev:'critical',sort:.5,action:'review',sourceId:p.id,memberId:p.memberId,canAck:true});
    });
  }catch(_e){}

  // Bolsa perto do fim: aviso tratável, não uma falsa conclusão acadêmica.
  scope.forEach(m=>{
    if(m.bolsista&&m.bolsaFim){
      const pi=trackPeriodInfo(m.bolsa

function ensureCss(){if(document.getElementById('p128Style'))return;const s=document.createElement('style');s.id='p128Style';s.textContent=css;document.head.appendChild(s)}

/* ---------- utilidades seguras ---------- */
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
function me(){try{return typeof myId!=='undefined'?myId:''}catch(_e){return''}}
function admin(){try{return !!isAdmin}catch(_e){return false}}
function st(){try{return state||{}}catch(_e){return{}}}
function ms(v){if(!v)return 0;try{if(typeof v.toMillis==='function')return v.toMillis();if(v.seconds)return v.seconds*1000;if(typeof v==='string'){if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v))return new Date(v+'T12:00:00').getTime();const t=Date.parse(v);return isNaN(t)?0:t}if(typeof v==='number')return v;if(v instanceof Date)return v.getTime()}catch(_e){}return 0}
function dayIso(d){const x=d||new Date();return x.getFullYear()+'-'+String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(x.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}
function fmtShort(t){if(!t)return'';return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})}
function ago(t){if(!t)return'nunca';const d=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/86400000);if(d<=0)return'hoje';if(d===1)return'ontem';if(d<30)return`${d} d`;const m=Math.floor(d/30);return m<12?`${m} m`:`${Math.floor(m/12)} a`}
function first(n){return String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/)[0]||'Carbonauta'}

/* ---------- 2) presença silenciosa ---------- */
let presenceDone=false;
async function markPresence(){
  const id=me();if(!id||presenceDone)return;
  const key='carbonautas_p128_seen_'+id,last=Number(localStorage.getItem(key)||0);
  if(Date.now()-last<30*60*1000){presenceDone=true;return}
  try{
    const f=FB();
    await f.updateDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{lastSeenAt:f.serverTimestamp(),lastSeenDay:dayIso()});
    localStorage.setItem(key,String(Date.now()));presenceDone=true;
  }catch(e){console.warn('P128 presença',e?.code||e)}
}

/* ---------- 3) painel de acessos (coordenação) ---------- */
function renderPresence(){
  const root=document.getElementById('dashAttention');
  if(!root||!admin()){const old=document.getElementById('p128Presence');if(old)old.remove();return}
  const members=(st().members||[]).filter(m=>m.status!=='egresso');
  if(!members.length)return;
  const rows=members.map(m=>({m,t:ms(m.lastSeenAt)||ms(m.lastSeenDay)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
  const cls=t=>{if(!t)return'nunca';const d=(Date.now()-t)/86400000;return d<1?'hoje':d<7?'semana':'longe'};
  const hoje=rows.filter(r=>cls(r.t)==='hoje').length;
  let box=document.getElementById('p128Presence');
  if(!box){box=document.createElement('section');box.id='p128Presence';root.parentNode.insertBefore(box,root)}
  const sig=rows.map(r=>r.m.id+':'+r.t).join('|');
  if(box.dataset.sig===sig)return;box.dataset.sig=sig;
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="p128-head">Acessos <small>${hoje} hoje · ${rows.length} pessoas</small></div><div class="p128-grid">${rows.map(r=>`<div class="p128-chip ${cls(r.t)}" title="${esc(r.m.nome||'')}"><i></i><b>${esc(first(r.m.nome))}</b><span>${ago(r.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 4) linha do tempo por pessoa ---------- */
const SHOW=12;
function timelineItems(m){
  const items=[];
  (m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.data);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:p.feito?'done':(t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`Prazo · ${p.titulo||''}`})});
  (st().activities||[]).filter(a=>a.ownerId===m.id).forEach(a=>{
    const t=ms(a.dueDate)||ms(a.updatedAt)||ms(a.createdAt);if(!t)return;
    const done=a.status==='concluido'||!!a.doneAt;
    items.push({t,cls:done?'done':(a.dueDate&&t<Date.now()?'over':''),txt:`${a.type==='produto'?'Produto':a.type==='orientacao'?'Orientação':'Tarefa'} · ${a.title||''}`})
  });
  (st().publicacoes||[]).filter(p=>p.memberId===m.id&&p.tipo==='arquivo').forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Arquivo · ${p.titulo||p.fileName||''}${p.reviewFlow?' · v'+(p.reviewVersion||1):''}`})});
  (st().photos||[]).filter(p=>p.autorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.dataRealizacao)||ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Foto · ${p.legenda||p.projeto||''}`})});
  (st().feed||[]).filter(p=>p.authorId===m.id).forEach(p=>{const t=ms(p.ts);if(!t)return;items.push({t,cls:'',txt:`Destaque · ${p.title||p.text||''}`})});
  return items.sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t);
}
